<link rel="alternate" type="text/markdown" href="/api/reference/overview/index.md"> <script type="module" src="/_astro/Head.astro_astro_type_script_index_0_lang.CWfk7Bko.js"></script><script>
  window.didInitThemePickers = window.didInitThemePickers ?? false;

  // Only run once.
  if (!window.didInitThemePickers) {
    window.didInitThemePickers = true;

    // The stored theme will be either 'auto', 'light', 'dark' or null.
    const storedTheme = typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' && localStorage.getItem('starlight-theme');

    // This theme is either 'light' or 'dark'. It's used for setting the data-theme attribute.
    const theme =
      !storedTheme || storedTheme === 'auto'
        ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)').matches
          ? 'light'
          : 'dark'
        : storedTheme;

    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';

    const themeSelects = document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-select]');
    const selectedThemeValue = storedTheme || 'auto';

    themeSelects.forEach((select) => {
      const tmpl = select.querySelector(`[data-value="${selectedThemeValue}"] template`);
      const selectedSlot = select.querySelector('[data-part="trigger-selected"]');

      selectedSlot.innerHTML = '';
      if (tmpl) {
        selectedSlot.appendChild(tmpl.content.cloneNode(true));
        return;
      }
    });
  }

class="flex flex-col gap-6 astro-3ef6ksr2">  </div> </div> </div> <div class="w-full px-6 py-6 border-t border-primary-surface astro-3ef6ksr2" data-mobile-nav-footer> <div class="flex flex-col gap-5 <script>
  document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("site-variant:apply"));
</script>  <script>
  const MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY = "mobile-nav:restore-open";

  const readPersistedMobileNavOpen = () => {
    try {
      return sessionStorage.getItem(MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY) === "true";
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const setPersistedMobileNavOpen = (isOpen) => {
    try {
      if (isOpen) {
        sessionStorage.setItem(MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY, "true");
      } else {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY);
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  function initializeMobileNavigation() {
    document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("site-variant:apply"));

    const drawer = document.getElementById("drawer");
    const drawerButton = document.getElementById("header-drawer-button");

    if (
      !drawer ||
      !drawerButton ||
      drawer.dataset.mobileNavInitialized === "true"
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const navTabElements = Array.from(
      drawer.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-nav-tab]")
    );
    const visibleNavTabElements = navTabElements.filter((tab) => !tab.hidden);
    const defaultSearchPlaceholder =
      drawer.dataset.defaultSearchPlaceholder || "Search the site";
    const defaultSearchScope = drawer.dataset.defaultSearchScope || "";
    const headerSearchOverlay = document.getElementById(
      "header-search-overlay"
    );
    const navLinkElements = Array.from(
      drawer.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-nav-link]")
    );
    const tabPanels = Array.from(
      drawer.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-nav-content]")
    );
    const isStarlightApiReferenceRoute =
      window.location.pathname === "/api/reference" ||
      window.location.pathname.startsWith("/api/reference/");
    const shouldRestoreDrawerOpen =
      matchMedia("(max-width: 63.999rem)").matches &&
      !isStarlightApiReferenceRoute &&
      readPersistedMobileNavOpen();

    const configuredDefaultTab = navTabElements.find(
      (tab) => tab.dataset.tabId === drawer.dataset.defaultTabId
    );
    let activeTabId =
      visibleNavTabElements.find((tab) => tab.dataset.isActive === "true")
        ?.dataset.tabId ||
      (!configuredDefaultTab?.hidden
        ? configuredDefaultTab?.dataset.tabId
        : undefined) ||
      visibleNavTabElements[0]?.dataset.tabId ||
      null;

    const updateSelectedOption = (tabId) => {
      let selectedLabel = "";
      let selectedPlaceholder = "";
      let selectedScope = "";

      navTabElements.forEach((tab) => {
        const isSelected = tab.dataset.tabId === tabId;
        tab.dataset.selected = isSelected ? "true" : "false";
        tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", isSelected ? "true" : "false");

        if (isSelected && !selectedLabel) {
          selectedLabel = tab.dataset.label || tab.textContent?.trim() || "";
        }

        if (isSelected && !selectedPlaceholder) {
          selectedPlaceholder = tab.dataset.searchPlaceholder || "";
        }

        if (isSelected && !selectedScope) {
          selectedScope = tab.dataset.searchScope || "";
        }
      });

      if (!selectedLabel && visibleNavTabElements[0]) {
        selectedLabel =
          visibleNavTabElements[0].dataset.label ||
          visibleNavTabElements[0].textContent?.trim() ||
          "";
      }

      if (!selectedPlaceholder && visibleNavTabElements[0]) {
        selectedPlaceholder =
          visibleNavTabElements[0].dataset.searchPlaceholder || "";
      }

      if (!selectedScope && visibleNavTabElements[0]) {
        selectedScope = visibleNavTabElements[0].dataset.searchScope || "";
      }

      const nextPlaceholder = selectedPlaceholder || defaultSearchPlaceholder;
      const nextScope = selectedScope || defaultSearchScope;
      const updatePlaceholder = (container) => {
        if (!container) return;
        const input = container.querySelector("[data-site-search-input]");
        if (input instanceof HTMLInputElement) {
          input.placeholder = nextPlaceholder;
        }
      };
      const updateScope = (container) => {
        if (!container) return;
        const target = container.querySelector("[data-site-search-root]");
        if (!target) return;
        target.setAttribute("data-scope", nextScope);
        target.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("site-search:update"));
      };
      updatePlaceholder(drawer);
      updatePlaceholder(headerSearchOverlay);
      updateScope(drawer);
      updateScope(headerSearchOverlay);
    };

    const activeVariantByTabId = new Map();

    const getTabLabel = (tabId) => {
      return (
        navTabElements.find((tab) => tab.dataset.tabId === tabId)?.dataset
          .label || ""
      );
    };

    const updatePanelBreadcrumb = (panel, tabId, contextLabel) => {
      const breadcrumb = panel.querySelector("[data-mobile-breadcrumb]");
      const parent = panel.querySelector("[data-mobile-breadcrumb-parent]");
      const childWrapper = panel.querySelector(
        "[data-mobile-breadcrumb-child-wrapper]"
      );
      const child = panel.querySelector("[data-mobile-breadcrumb-child]");
      const contextOptions = panel.querySelector(
        "[data-mobile-context-options]"
      );

      if (contextOptions) {
        contextOptions.dataset.contextActive = contextLabel ? "true" : "false";
      }

      if (!breadcrumb || !parent || !childWrapper || !child) {
        return;
      }

      const tabLabel = getTabLabel(tabId);
      parent.textContent = tabLabel;

      if (!contextLabel) {
        breadcrumb.setAttribute("hidden", "true");
        childWrapper.setAttribute("hidden", "true");
        child.textContent = "";
        return;
      }

      breadcrumb.removeAttribute("hidden");
      childWrapper.removeAttribute("hidden");
      child.textContent = contextLabel;
    };

    const selectVariantForPanel = (panel, tabId, variantId) => {
      if (!variantId) {
        updatePanelBreadcrumb(panel, tabId, "");
        return;
      }

      const contextOptions = Array.from(
        panel.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-context-option]")
      );
      const contextSelects = Array.from(
        panel.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-context-select]")
      );
      let selectedContextLabel = "";

      contextOptions.forEach((option) => {
        const isSelected = option.dataset.contextId === variantId;
        option.dataset.selected = isSelected ? "true" : "false";
        if (isSelected) {
          selectedContextLabel = option.dataset.contextLabel || "";
        }
      });

      contextSelects.forEach((select) => {
        select.dataset.value = variantId;
        select.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("mobile-context-select-sync", {
            detail: { value: variantId },
          })
        );
      });

      const variantSections = Array.from(
        panel.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-nav-variant-content]")
      );
      variantSections.forEach((section) => {
        const isSelected = section.dataset.variantId === variantId;
        if (isSelected) {
          section.removeAttribute("hidden");
        } else {
          section.setAttribute("hidden", "true");
        }
      });

      updatePanelBreadcrumb(panel, tabId, selectedContextLabel);
      activeVariantByTabId.set(tabId, variantId);
    };

    const activateTab = (tabId) => {
      if (!tabId) return;
      activeTabId = tabId;
      updateSelectedOption(tabId);

      tabPanels.forEach((panel) => {
        const panelTabId = panel.getAttribute("data-tab-id");
        const isActive = panelTabId === tabId;
        if (isActive) {
          panel.removeAttribute("hidden");
          const defaultVariantId = panel.getAttribute(
            "data-default-variant-id"
          );
          const nextVariantId =
            activeVariantByTabId.get(tabId) ||
            defaultVariantId ||
            panel.querySelector("[data-mobile-nav-variant-content]")?.dataset
              .variantId ||
            "";
          selectVariantForPanel(panel, tabId, nextVariantId);
        } else {
          panel.setAttribute("hidden", "true");
        }
      });
    };

    const closeDrawer = () => {
      drawer.classList.remove("open");
      drawerButton.classList.remove("open");
      drawerButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
      setPersistedMobileNavOpen(false);
    };

    const openDrawer = () => {
      drawer.classList.add("open");
      drawerButton.classList.add("open");
      drawerButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
      if (activeTabId) {
        activateTab(activeTabId);
      }
    };

    const toggleDrawer = () => {
      if (drawer.classList.contains("open")) {
        closeDrawer();
      } else {
        openDrawer();
      }
    };

    const handleTabSelection = (tab) => {
      const hasNav = tab.dataset.hasNav === "true";
      const href = tab.dataset.href;
      const tabId = tab.dataset.tabId;

      if (!tabId) {
        return;
      }

      if (!hasNav && href) {
        setPersistedMobileNavOpen(true);
        window.location.href = href;
        return;
      }

      activateTab(tabId);
    };

    drawerButton.addEventListener("click", toggleDrawer);

    navTabElements.forEach((tab) => {
      tab.addEventListener("click", () => {
        handleTabSelection(tab);
      });

      tab.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        if (!visibleNavTabElements.length) return;

        const currentIndex = visibleNavTabElements.indexOf(tab);

        if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
          event.preventDefault();
          const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % visibleNavTabElements.length;
          visibleNavTabElements[nextIndex]?.focus();
        } else if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
          event.preventDefault();
          const prevIndex =
            (currentIndex - 1 + visibleNavTabElements.length) %
            visibleNavTabElements.length;
          visibleNavTabElements[prevIndex]?.focus();
        } else if (event.key === "Home") {
          event.preventDefault();
          visibleNavTabElements[0]?.focus();
        } else if (event.key === "End") {
          event.preventDefault();
          visibleNavTabElements[visibleNavTabElements.length - 1]?.focus();
        } else if (
          event.key === "Enter" ||
          event.key === " " ||
          event.key === "Space" ||
          event.key === "Spacebar"
        ) {
          event.preventDefault();
          handleTabSelection(tab);
        } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
          event.preventDefault();
          closeDrawer();
          drawerButton.focus();
        }
      });
    });

    tabPanels.forEach((panel) => {
      const tabId = panel.getAttribute("data-tab-id") || "";
      const contextOptions = Array.from(
        panel.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-context-option]")
      );

      contextOptions.forEach((option) => {
        option.addEventListener("click", () => {
          const contextHref = option.dataset.contextHref;
          if (
            contextHref &&
            contextHref.startsWith("/api/reference") &&
            tabId
          ) {
            closeDrawer();
            window.location.href = contextHref;
            return;
          }

          const variantId = option.dataset.contextId;
          if (!variantId || !tabId) {
            return;
          }

          selectVariantForPanel(panel, tabId, variantId);
        });
      });

      const contextSelects = Array.from(
        panel.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-context-select]")
      );
      contextSelects.forEach((select) => {
        select.addEventListener("mobile-context-select-change", (event) => {
          if (!(event instanceof CustomEvent) || !tabId) {
            return;
          }

          const variantId = event.detail?.value;
          if (typeof variantId !== "string") {
            return;
          }

          selectVariantForPanel(panel, tabId, variantId);
        });
      });
    });

    navLinkElements.forEach((link) => {
      link.addEventListener("click", () => {
        closeDrawer();
      });
    });

    const mobileSearch = drawer.querySelector("[data-mobile-search]");
    mobileSearch?.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
      const target = event.target;
      if (target instanceof Element) {
        const anchor = target.closest("a[href]");
        if (anchor) {
          closeDrawer();
        }
      }
    });

    mobileSearch?.addEventListener("focusin", (event) => {
      const target = event.target;
      if (!(target instanceof HTMLInputElement) || target.type !== "text") {
        return;
      }
      closeDrawer();
      window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (document.activeElement === target) {
          target.blur();
        }
        document.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("header:open-search", {
            detail: {
              trigger: target,
              variant: "mobile",
            },
          })
        );
      });
    });

    drawer.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        closeDrawer();
        drawerButton.focus();
      }
    });

    drawer.dataset.mobileNavInitialized = "true";
    if (activeTabId) {
      activateTab(activeTabId);
    }

    if (shouldRestoreDrawerOpen) {
      openDrawer();
      setPersistedMobileNavOpen(false);
    }
  }

  function initializeHeaderSearch() {
    const overlay = document.getElementById("header-search-overlay");
    if (!overlay) {
      return;
    }

    const getSearchButtons = () =>
      Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-header-search-button]"));

    const closeButtons = overlay.querySelectorAll("[data-header-search-close]");
    const dismissTarget = overlay.querySelector("[data-header-search-dismiss]");
    const panel = overlay.querySelector("[data-header-search-panel]");
    const overlayMobileClass = "header-search-overlay--mobile";
    const panelMobileClass = "header-search-panel--mobile";
    let lastTrigger = null;
    let lastVariant = null;

    const setExpandedState = (isOpen) => {
      const expanded = isOpen ? "true" : "false";
      getSearchButtons().forEach((button) => {
        button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", expanded);
        button.setAttribute("data-active", expanded);
      });
      overlay.dataset.open = expanded;
      overlay.setAttribute("aria-hidden", isOpen ? "false" : "true");
    };

    const focusSearchInput = () => {
      window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const input = overlay.querySelector("[data-site-search-input]");
        if (input) {
          input.focus();
          input.select();
        }
      });
    };

    const openOverlay = (trigger, options = {}) => {
      lastTrigger = trigger ?? document.activeElement;
      const variant = options.variant ?? null;
      lastVariant = typeof variant === "string" ? variant : null;
      overlay.classList.remove("hidden");
      overlay.classList.add("flex");
      const isMobileVariant = lastVariant === "mobile";
      if (isMobileVariant) {
        overlay.dataset.variant = "mobile";
      } else {
        delete overlay.dataset.variant;
      }
      overlay.classList.toggle(overlayMobileClass, isMobileVariant);
      panel?.classList.toggle(panelMobileClass, isMobileVariant);
      document.documentElement.classList.add("has-header-search-open");
      setExpandedState(true);
      focusSearchInput();
    };

    const closeOverlay = () => {
      overlay.classList.add("hidden");
      overlay.classList.remove("flex");
      document.documentElement.classList.remove("has-header-search-open");
      overlay.classList.remove(overlayMobileClass);
      panel?.classList.remove(panelMobileClass);
      delete overlay.dataset.variant;
      setExpandedState(false);
      if (lastTrigger instanceof HTMLElement) {
        if (
          lastVariant === "mobile" &&
          typeof lastTrigger.blur === "function"
        ) {
          lastTrigger.blur();
        } else if (lastVariant !== "mobile") {
          lastTrigger.focus();
        }
      }
      lastTrigger = null;
      lastVariant = null;
    };

    const bindSearchButtons = () => {
      getSearchButtons().forEach((button) => {
        if (button.dataset.searchButtonInitialized === "true") {
          return;
        }

        button.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          openOverlay(button);
        });

        button.dataset.searchButtonInitialized = "true";
      });
    };

    if (overlay.dataset.searchInitialized !== "true") {
      closeButtons.forEach((button) => {
        button.addEventListener("click", () => {
          closeOverlay();
        });
      });

      overlay.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        if (event.target === overlay) {
          closeOverlay();
        }
      });

      dismissTarget?.addEventListener("click", closeOverlay);

      const handleKeydown = (event) => {
        const key = "key" in event ? event.key : undefined;
        const isShortcut =
          !!key &&
          key.toLowerCase() === "k" &&
          (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey);

        if (isShortcut) {
          event.preventDefault();
          const buttons = getSearchButtons();
          openOverlay(buttons[0] ?? null);
          return;
        }

        if (key === "Escape" && overlay.dataset.open === "true") {
          event.preventDefault();
          closeOverlay();
        }
      };

      document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeydown);

      document.addEventListener("header:open-search", (event) => {
        const detail =
          event instanceof CustomEvent && typeof event.detail === "object"
            ? event.detail
            : {};
        const trigger =
          detail && detail.trigger instanceof HTMLElement
            ? detail.trigger
            : null;
        openOverlay(trigger, detail);
      });

      document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", () => {
        if (overlay.dataset.open === "true") {
          closeOverlay();
        }
      });

      overlay.dataset.searchInitialized = "true";
    }

    bindSearchButtons();
  }

  const handleAfterSwap = () => {
    initializeMobileNavigation();
    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      initializeHeaderSearch();
    });
  };

  document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", handleAfterSwap);
  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", handleAfterSwap, {
      once: true,
    });
  } else {
    handleAfterSwap();
  }
</script> <script>(()=>{var e=async t=>{await(await t())()};(self.Astro||(self.Astro={})).only=e;window.dispatchEvent(new Event("astro:only"));})();</script><astro-island uid="DQ4jb" </a> </nav> </div> <script src="/js/theme.js"></script> <script type="module" src="/_astro/StarlightHeader.astro_astro_type_script_index_0_lang.BAYPrJlt.js"></script> <script>
  const STARLIGHT_MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY = "mobile-nav:restore-open";
  const STARLIGHT_MENU_TOGGLE_SELECTOR = "starlight-menu-button";
  const STARLIGHT_PRIMARY_TAB_SELECTOR =
    "#starlight-mobile-primary-tabs a[href]";
  const STARLIGHT_API_SECTION_NAV_SELECTOR =
    '#starlight-mobile-primary-tabs nav[aria-label="API sections"]';
  const AI_DROPDOWN_SELECTOR = '[data-dropdown-id="ai-dropdown-button"]';
  const DISABLED_AI_OPTIONS = ["open-in-claude", "open-in-cursor"];
  const STARLIGHT_THEME_BUTTON_ID = "starlight-mobile-theme-button";

  const readStarlightPersistedMobileNavOpen = () => {
    try {
      return (
        sessionStorage.getItem(STARLIGHT_MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY) === "true"
      );
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const setStarlightPersistedMobileNavOpen = (isOpen) => {
    try {
      if (isOpen) {
        sessionStorage.setItem(STARLIGHT_MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY, "true");
      } else {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(STARLIGHT_MOBILE_NAV_PERSIST_KEY);
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  const revealActiveMobileApiSection = () => {
    const nav = document.querySelector(STARLIGHT_API_SECTION_NAV_SELECTOR);
    if (!(nav instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    const activeLink = nav.querySelector('[aria-current="true"]');
    if (!(activeLink instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    nav.scrollLeft = Math.max(
      0,
      activeLink.offsetLeft - (nav.clientWidth - activeLink.clientWidth) / 2
    );
  };

  const removeDisabledAIDropdownOptions = () => {
    const dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll(AI_DROPDOWN_SELECTOR);
    if (!dropdowns.length) return;

    dropdowns.forEach((dropdown) => {
      DISABLED_AI_OPTIONS.forEach((id) => {
        const option = dropdown.querySelector(`[data-value="${id}"]`);
        option?.remove();
      });
    });
  };

  const wireAIDropdownOptionCleanup = () => {
    removeDisabledAIDropdownOptions();
    // The Stainless dropdown wiring can re-render items on navigation/hydration.
    window.setTimeout(removeDisabledAIDropdownOptions, 0);
  };

  const openStarlightMobileMenu = () => {
    const menuToggle = document.querySelector(STARLIGHT_MENU_TOGGLE_SELECTOR);
    if (!(menuToggle instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
    document.body.toggleAttribute("data-mobile-menu-expanded", true);
    window.requestAnimationFrame(revealActiveMobileApiSection);
    return true;
  };

  const wireActiveMobileApiSection = () => {
    const menuToggle = document.querySelector(STARLIGHT_MENU_TOGGLE_SELECTOR);
    if (!(menuToggle instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    if (menuToggle.dataset.apiSectionScrollBound === "true") return;

    menuToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {
      window.requestAnimationFrame(revealActiveMobileApiSection);
    });
    menuToggle.dataset.apiSectionScrollBound = "true";
  };

  const restoreStarlightMobileMenu = (attempt = 0) => {
    if (openStarlightMobileMenu()) {
      setStarlightPersistedMobileNavOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    if (attempt >= 12) {
      setStarlightPersistedMobileNavOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      restoreStarlightMobileMenu(attempt + 1);
    });
  };

  const wireMobileNavPersistence = () => {
    const tabLinks = document.querySelectorAll(STARLIGHT_PRIMARY_TAB_SELECTOR);
    tabLinks.forEach((link) => {
      if (!(link instanceof HTMLAnchorElement)) return;
      if (link.dataset.mobileNavPersistBound === "true") return;
      link.addEventListener("click", () => {
        setStarlightPersistedMobileNavOpen(true);
      });
      link.dataset.mobileNavPersistBound = "true";
    });

    if (!matchMedia("(max-width: 50rem)").matches) return;
    if (!readStarlightPersistedMobileNavOpen()) return;
    restoreStarlightMobileMenu();
  };

  const wireStarlightThemeButton = () => {
    const themeButton = document.getElementById(STARLIGHT_THEME_BUTTON_ID);
    if (!(themeButton instanceof HTMLButtonElement)) return;
    if (themeButton.dataset.themeBound === "true") return;

    themeButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
      if (typeof changeTheme === "function") {
        changeTheme();
      }
    });
    themeButton.dataset.themeBound = "true";
  };

  const wireStarlightHeaderEnhancements = () => {
    document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("site-variant:apply"));
    wireAIDropdownOptionCleanup();
    wireMobileNavPersistence();
    wireActiveMobileApiSection();
    wireStarlightThemeButton();
  };

  document.addEventListener(
    "DOMContentLoaded",
    wireStarlightHeaderEnhancements
  );
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", wireStarlightHeaderEnhancements);
  document.addEventListener(
    "astro:after-swap",
    wireStarlightHeaderEnhancements
  );

  wireStarlightHeaderEnhancements();
</script> </header> <nav class="sidebar" aria-label="Main"> <starlight-menu-button class="print:hidden astro-pcts6iom"> <button aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Menu" aria-controls="starlight__sidebar" class="sl-flex md:sl-hidden astro-pcts6iom"> <svg aria-hidden="true" class="open-menu astro-pcts6iom astro-rgdbnx6k" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" style="--sl-icon-size: 1em;"><path d="M3 8h18a1 1 0 1 0 0-2H3a1 1 0 0 0 0 2Zm18 8H3a1 1 0 0 0 0 2h18a1 1 0 0 0 0-2Zm0-5H3a1 1 0 0 0 0 2h18a1 1 0 0 0 0-2Z"/></svg> <svg aria-hidden="true" class="close-menu astro-pcts6iom astro-rgdbnx6k" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" style="--sl-icon-size: 1em;"><path d="m13.41 12 6.3-6.29a1.004 1.004 0 1 0-1.42-1.42L12 10.59l-6.29-6.3a1.004 1.004 0 0 0-1.42 1.42l6.3 6.29-6.3 6.29a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42.998.998 0 0 0 1.42 0l6.29-6.3 6.29 6.3a.999.999 0 0 0 1.42 0 1 1 0 0 0 0-1.42L13.41 12Z"/></svg> </button> </starlight-menu-button> <script type="module">class s extends stldocs-sidebar-entry-group"><details class="stldocs-sidebar-expander"><summary class="stldocs-expander-summary"><span>Completions</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-right stldocs-icon stldocs-expander-summary-icon" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m9 18 6-6-6-6"></path></svg></summary><ul class="stldocs-sidebar-list"><li class="stldocs-sidebar-entry stldocs-sidebar-entry-link"><a href="/api/reference/resources/completions/methods/create" data-stldocs-method="post"><span class="stl-ui-badge stl-ui-badge--size-sm stl-sidebar-icon stl-ui-badge--http stl-ui-badge--http-post" role="img" aria-label="POST"></span>Create a completion</a></li></ul></details></li></ul></div></li></ul></div> <script>
  const SIDEBAR_CONTAINER_SELECTOR = "#starlight__sidebar";
  const ACTIVE_ITEM_SELECTOR =
    "a[aria-current='page'], [aria-current='page'] a";
  const STORAGE_KEY = "starlight-sidebar-scroll";
  const INITIALIZED_ATTRIBUTE = "data-sidebar-scroll-initialized";
  const OVERVIEW_PATH = "/api/reference/overview";
  const OVERVIEW_INTRODUCTION_ID = "introduction";
  const MAIN_FRAME_SELECTOR = ".page > .main-frame, .page";
  const OVERVIEW_SECTION_SCROLL_OFFSET = 12;
  const OVERVIEW_HASH_PRIORITY_MS = 900;
  const MODEL_ANCHOR_LINK_SELECTOR =
    "a[href*='/api/reference/resources/'][href*='#(resource)']";

  let disposeOverviewSectionObserver = null;

  const isStorageAvailable = (() => {
    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem(`${STORAGE_KEY}-test`, "1");
      sessionStorage.removeItem(`${STORAGE_KEY}-test`);
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  })();

  const getSidebarContainer = () => {
    const container = document.querySelector(SIDEBAR_CONTAINER_SELECTOR);
    return container instanceof HTMLElement ? container : null;
  };

  const saveScrollPosition = (container) => {
    if (!isStorageAvailable) return;
    sessionStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, String(container.scrollTop));
  };

  const restoreScrollPosition = (container) => {
    if (!isStorageAvailable) return false;

    const storedScrollTop = sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (storedScrollTop === null) return false;

    const parsedScrollTop = Number(storedScrollTop);
    if (!Number.isFinite(parsedScrollTop)) return false;

    container.scrollTop = parsedScrollTop;
    return true;
  };

  const isVisibleInContainer = (element, container) => {
    const elementRect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
    const containerRect = container.getBoundingClientRect();

    return (
      elementRect.bottom > containerRect.top &&
      elementRect.top < containerRect.bottom
    );
  };

  const scrollActiveSidebarItemToMiddle = (container, activeItem) => {
    const targetTop =
      activeItem.getBoundingClientRect().top -
      container.getBoundingClientRect().top +
      container.scrollTop -
      container.clientHeight / 2 +
      activeItem.clientHeight / 2;

    container.scrollTo({
      top: Math.max(0, targetTop),
      behavior: "auto",
    });
  };

  const setupSidebar = () => {
    const container = getSidebarContainer();
    if (!container || container.getAttribute(INITIALIZED_ATTRIBUTE) === "true")
      return;

    container.addEventListener(
      "scroll",
      () => {
        saveScrollPosition(container);
      },
      { passive: true }
    );

    container.setAttribute(INITIALIZED_ATTRIBUTE, "true");
  };

  const persistScrollPosition = () => {
    const container = getSidebarContainer();
    if (!container) return;
    saveScrollPosition(container);
  };

  const restoreAndMaybeCenter = () => {
    const container = getSidebarContainer();
    if (!container) return;

    const hasStoredScrollPosition = restoreScrollPosition(container);
    if (!matchMedia("(min-width: 50em)").matches) return;

    const activeItem = container.querySelector(ACTIVE_ITEM_SELECTOR);
    if (!(activeItem instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    const shouldCenter =
      !hasStoredScrollPosition || !isVisibleInContainer(activeItem, container);

    if (shouldCenter) {
      scrollActiveSidebarItemToMiddle(container, activeItem);
    }
  };

  const initialize = () => {
    setupSidebar();
    setupOverviewSectionHighlight();
    syncModelAnchorSidebarActiveState();
    // Wait a frame so sidebar DOM/layout settles after client navigation.
    requestAnimationFrame(restoreAndMaybeCenter);
  };

  const normalizePathname = (pathname) => pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "") || "/";
  const normalizeHash = (hash) =>
    decodeURIComponent(hash.replace(/^#/, "")).trim();
  const prefersReducedMotion = () =>
    window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

  const isScrollableElement = (element) => {
    if (!(element instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;

    const style = window.getComputedStyle(element);
    const overflowY = style.overflowY;
    const allowsScroll =
      overflowY === "auto" || overflowY === "scroll" || overflowY === "overlay";

    return allowsScroll && element.scrollHeight > element.clientHeight + 1;
  };

  const getMainScrollContainer = () => {
    const candidates = document.querySelectorAll(MAIN_FRAME_SELECTOR);
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      if (isScrollableElement(candidate)) {
        return candidate;
      }
    }

    return null;
  };

  const scrollOverviewHeadingIntoView = (heading) => {
    const behavior = prefersReducedMotion() ? "auto" : "smooth";
    const mainFrame = getMainScrollContainer();

    if (mainFrame instanceof HTMLElement) {
      const targetTop =
        heading.getBoundingClientRect().top -
        mainFrame.getBoundingClientRect().top +
        mainFrame.scrollTop -
        OVERVIEW_SECTION_SCROLL_OFFSET;

      mainFrame.scrollTo({
        top: Math.max(0, targetTop),
        behavior,
      });
      return;
    }

    heading.scrollIntoView({ behavior, block: "start" });
  };

  const syncModelAnchorSidebarActiveState = () => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

    const sidebarContainer = getSidebarContainer();
    if (!sidebarContainer) return;

    const currentPathname = normalizePathname(window.location.pathname);
    const currentHash = normalizeHash(window.location.hash);
    const hasCurrentHash = currentHash.length > 0;

    const modelAnchorLinks = Array.from(
      sidebarContainer.querySelectorAll(MODEL_ANCHOR_LINK_SELECTOR)
    ).filter((link) => link instanceof HTMLAnchorElement);

    modelAnchorLinks.forEach((link) => {
      const linkUrl = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
      const linkPath = normalizePathname(linkUrl.pathname);
      const linkHash = normalizeHash(linkUrl.hash);
      const isActive =
        hasCurrentHash &&
        linkPath === currentPathname &&
        linkHash === currentHash;

      if (isActive) {
        link.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
      } else {
        link.removeAttribute("aria-current");
      }
    });
  };

  const setupOverviewSectionHighlight = () => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

    disposeOverviewSectionObserver?.();
    disposeOverviewSectionObserver = null;

    if (
      normalizePathname(window.location.pathname) !==
      normalizePathname(OVERVIEW_PATH)
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const sidebarContainer = getSidebarContainer();
    if (!sidebarContainer) return;

    const sectionLinks = Array.from(
      sidebarContainer.querySelectorAll("a[href]")
    ).filter((link) => {
      if (!(link instanceof HTMLAnchorElement)) return false;
      const url = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
      return (
        normalizePathname(url.pathname) === normalizePathname(OVERVIEW_PATH)
      );
    });

    if (sectionLinks.length <= 1) return;

    const introLink = sectionLinks.find((link) => {
      const url = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
      return !url.hash;
    });
    const hashedLinks = sectionLinks.filter((link) => {
      const url = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
      return Boolean(url.hash);
    });

    const sectionTargets = [];
    if (introLink) {
      const introHeading =
        document.getElementById(OVERVIEW_INTRODUCTION_ID) ??
        document.querySelector("main h2[id]");
      if (introHeading instanceof HTMLElement) {
        sectionTargets.push({
          id: introHeading.id,
          heading: introHeading,
          link: introLink,
        });
      }
    }

    for (const link of hashedLinks) {
      const url = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
      const id = decodeURIComponent(url.hash.slice(1));
      const heading = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!(heading instanceof HTMLElement)) continue;
      sectionTargets.push({ id, heading, link });
    }

    if (sectionTargets.length <= 1) return;

    let bottomSentinelVisible = false;
    let hashPriorityUntil = 0;
    const intersectionState = new Map();
    const targetIndexById = new Map(
      sectionTargets.map(({ id }, index) => [id, index])
    );
    const introTargetIndex = introLink
      ? sectionTargets.findIndex(({ link }) => link === introLink)
      : -1;

    const applyActiveLink = (activeIndex) => {
      sectionTargets.forEach(({ link }, index) => {
        if (index === activeIndex) {
          link.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
        } else {
          link.removeAttribute("aria-current");
        }
      });
    };

    const getActiveIndex = () => {
      if (bottomSentinelVisible) return sectionTargets.length - 1;

      const activationOffset = Math.min(window.innerHeight * 0.3, 180);
      let index = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < sectionTargets.length; i += 1) {
        const { heading } = sectionTargets[i];
        if (heading.getBoundingClientRect().top <= activationOffset) {
          index = i;
          continue;
        }
        break;
      }

      return index;
    };

    const applyActiveLinkFromLocation = () => {
      const currentHash = normalizeHash(window.location.hash);
      if (!currentHash) return false;

      const hashMatchIndex = targetIndexById.get(currentHash);
      if (typeof hashMatchIndex === "number") {
        applyActiveLink(hashMatchIndex);
        return true;
      }

      return false;
    };

    const refreshHashPriorityWindow = () => {
      hashPriorityUntil = performance.now() + OVERVIEW_HASH_PRIORITY_MS;
    };

    const updateActiveLink = () => {
      if (
        performance.now() < hashPriorityUntil &&
        applyActiveLinkFromLocation()
      ) {
        return;
      }

      const hasIntersectingSection =
        bottomSentinelVisible ||
        Array.from(intersectionState.values()).some(
          (isIntersecting) => isIntersecting
        );
      if (!hasIntersectingSection) return;

      applyActiveLink(getActiveIndex());
    };

    const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        for (const entry of entries) {
          intersectionState.set(entry.target, entry.isIntersecting);
        }
        updateActiveLink();
      },
      {
        root: null,
        threshold: 0,
        rootMargin: "-20% 0px -65% 0px",
      }
    );

    for (const { heading } of sectionTargets) {
      intersectionState.set(heading, false);
      sectionObserver.observe(heading);
    }

    const bottomSentinel = document.createElement("div");
    bottomSentinel.setAttribute("data-api-overview-bottom-sentinel", "true");
    bottomSentinel.style.cssText =
      "position: relative; width: 1px; height: 1px; pointer-events: none;";

    const mainContent =
      document.querySelector("main .sl-markdown-content") ??
      document.querySelector("main");
    if (!(mainContent instanceof HTMLElement)) {
      sectionObserver.disconnect();
      return;
    }
    mainContent.append(bottomSentinel);

    const bottomObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        bottomSentinelVisible = entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting);
        updateActiveLink();
      },
      {
        root: null,
        threshold: 0,
      }
    );
    bottomObserver.observe(bottomSentinel);

    const onOverviewHashChange = () => {
      refreshHashPriorityWindow();
      applyActiveLinkFromLocation();
    };
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", onOverviewHashChange);

    const onOverviewLinkClick = (event) => {
      if (
        event.defaultPrevented ||
        event.button !== 0 ||
        event.metaKey ||
        event.ctrlKey ||
        event.shiftKey ||
        event.altKey
      ) {
        return;
      }

      const link = event.currentTarget;
      if (!(link instanceof HTMLAnchorElement)) return;

      const linkUrl = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
      if (
        normalizePathname(linkUrl.pathname) !== normalizePathname(OVERVIEW_PATH)
      ) {
        return;
      }

      const targetId =
        normalizeHash(linkUrl.hash) ||
        sectionTargets[introTargetIndex >= 0 ? introTargetIndex : 0]?.id;
      if (!targetId) return;

      const targetIndex = targetIndexById.get(targetId);
      if (typeof targetIndex !== "number") return;

      const { heading } = sectionTargets[targetIndex];
      if (!(heading instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

      event.preventDefault();
      applyActiveLink(targetIndex);
      scrollOverviewHeadingIntoView(heading);
      refreshHashPriorityWindow();

      history.pushState(
        null,
        "",
        `${linkUrl.pathname}${linkUrl.search}${linkUrl.hash}`
      );
    };

    for (const { link } of sectionTargets) {
      link.addEventListener("click", onOverviewLinkClick);
    }

    applyActiveLinkFromLocation();
    updateActiveLink();

    disposeOverviewSectionObserver = () => {
      sectionObserver.disconnect();
      bottomObserver.disconnect();
      bottomSentinel.remove();
      window.removeEventListener("hashchange", onOverviewHashChange);
      for (const { link } of sectionTargets) {
        link.removeEventListener("click", onOverviewLinkClick);
      }
    };
  };

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initialize);
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", initialize);
  document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", initialize);
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", syncModelAnchorSidebarActiveState);

  document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", persistScrollPosition);
  document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", () => {
    disposeOverviewSectionObserver?.();
    disposeOverviewSectionObserver = null;
  });
  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", persistScrollPosition);
  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", () => {
    disposeOverviewSectionObserver?.();
    disposeOverviewSectionObserver = null;
  });

  initialize();
</script> </div> </nav>      <script type="module">const a=document.getElementById("starlight__sidebar"),n=a?.querySelector("sl-sidebar-state-persist"),o="sl-sidebar-state",i=()=>{let t=[];const e=n?.dataset.hash||"";try{const   <main data-pagefind-body class="astro-jftc2ajk" lang="en" dir="ltr">    <div class="stl-ui-prose stl-content-panel astro-efu33vlz">  <div class="stl-ui-not-prose stl-page-nav-container stl-prose-page-nav-container astro-2m32effb" data-pagefind-ignore><div class="stl-content-breadcrumbs stldocs-root"><div class="stldocs-breadcrumbs"><div class="stldocs-breadcrumbs-item"><span class="stldocs-breadcrumbs-non-link">API Reference</span></div><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-right" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m9 18 6-6-6-6"></path></svg><div class="stldocs-breadcrumbs-item"><a href="/api/reference/overview" class="stldocs-breadcrumbs-link">Introduction</a></div></div></div></div><h1 id="_top" class="astro-5padttu7">API Overview</h1>  </div> <div class="stl-ui-prose stl-content-panel astro-efu33vlz">  <div class="sl-markdown-content">  <div class="api-reference-custom-content" data-api-anchor-links data-api-anchor-offset="28" data-api-anchor-behavior="instant"> <p>Use this reference to look up OpenAI API endpoints, request and response
schemas, streaming events, client library methods, and shared behavior such as
authentication, errors, rate limits, and request IDs.</p>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="start-here">Start here</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#start-here"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore="true">Section titled “Start here”</span></a></div>
<ol>
<li>Choose the API surface for your application:
<ul>
<li><a href="/api/reference/responses/overview">Responses</a> for direct model requests, tool use, audio, image, and text inputs, and stateful interactions.</li>
<li><a href="/api/docs/guides/realtime">Realtime API</a> for low-latency voice or audio sessions over WebRTC, WebSocket, or SIP. Use the <a href="/api/reference/resources/realtime/client-events">client events</a> and <a href="/api/reference/resources/realtime/server-events">server events</a> references while building sessions.</li>
<li><a href="/api/reference/administration/overview">Administration</a> for organization workflows such as users, invites, projects, API keys, and audit logs.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Create credentials. Use a standard <a href="https://platform.openai.com/settings/organization/api-keys">API key</a> for application requests, an <a href="https://platform.openai.com/settings/organization/admin-keys">Admin API key</a> for Administration endpoints, or <a href="/api/docs/guides/workload-identity-federation">workload identity federation</a> for short-lived access tokens.</li>
<li>Install an official client library from the <a href="/api/docs/libraries">libraries page</a>, or call the HTTP API directly from any environment that supports HTTP requests.</li>
<li>Make a first request with the <a href="/api/docs/quickstart">developer quickstart</a> or go straight to the <a href="/api/reference/resources/responses/methods/create">Responses create reference</a>.</li>
<li>Before production, review <a href="/api/docs/guides/error-codes">error codes</a>, <a href="/api/docs/guides/rate-limits">rate limits</a>, and request ID logging below.</li>
</ol>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="authentication">Authentication</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#authentication"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore="true">Section titled “Authentication”</span></a></div>
<p>The OpenAI API accepts bearer credentials from API keys or from short-lived access tokens created with <a href="/api/docs/guides/workload-identity-federation">workload identity federation</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Remember that your API key is a secret.</strong> Don’t share it with others or expose it in any client-side code such as browsers or apps. Load API keys from an environment variable or key management service on the server.</p>
<p>Provide API credentials with <a href="https://swagger.io/docs/specification/v3_0/authentication/bearer-authentication/">HTTP Bearer authentication</a>.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="bash" dir="ltr"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Authorization:</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> Bearer</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> OPENAI_API_KEY_OR_ACCESS_TOKEN</span></span></code></pre>
<p>If you belong to more than one organization or access projects through a legacy user API key, pass a header to specify which organization and project to use for an API request:</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="bash" dir="ltr"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.openai.com/v1/models</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;Authorization: Bearer </span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">$OPENAI_API_KEY</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;OpenAI-Organization: </span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">$ORGANIZATION_ID</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;OpenAI-Project: </span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">$PROJECT_ID</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Usage from these API requests counts as usage for the specified organization and project. Find organization and project IDs in your <a href="https://platform.openai.com/settings/organization/general">dashboard settings</a>.</p>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="debugging-requests">Debugging requests</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#debugging-requests"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore="true">Section titled “Debugging requests”</span></a></div>
<p><a href="/api/docs/guides/error-codes">Error codes</a> describe failures returned from API responses. Inspect HTTP response headers for the unique ID of a request and rate limit details. Common response headers include:</p>
<p><strong>API meta information</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><code dir="auto">openai-organization</code>: The <a href="/api/docs/guides/production-best-practices#setting-up-your-organization">organization</a> associated with the request</li>
<li><code dir="auto">openai-processing-ms</code>: Time taken processing your API request</li>
<li><code dir="auto">openai-version</code>: REST API version used for this request (currently <code dir="auto">2020-10-01</code>)</li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-request-id</code>: Unique identifier for this API request (used in troubleshooting)</li>
</ul>
<p><strong><a href="/api/docs/guides/rate-limits">Rate limiting information</a></strong></p>
<ul>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-limit-requests</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-limit-tokens</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-remaining-requests</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-remaining-tokens</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-reset-requests</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-reset-tokens</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-limit-project-tokens</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-remaining-project-tokens</code></li>
<li><code dir="auto">x-ratelimit-reset-project-tokens</code></li>
</ul>
<p>Project-token headers may be present when a project-scoped token limit applies.</p>
<p><strong>OpenAI recommends logging request IDs in production deployments</strong> for more efficient troubleshooting with the <a href="https://help.openai.com/en/">support team</a>, should the need arise. Official <a href="/api/docs/libraries">client libraries</a> provide a property on top-level response objects containing the value of the <code dir="auto">x-request-id</code> header.</p>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="supplying-your-own-request-id-with-x-client-request-id">Supplying your own request ID with <code dir="auto">X-Client-Request-Id</code></h3><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#supplying-your-own-request-id-with-x-client-request-id"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore="true">Section titled “Supplying your own request ID with X-Client-Request-Id”</span></a></div>
<p>Along with the server-generated <code dir="auto">x-request-id</code>, you can supply your own unique identifier for each request via the <code dir="auto">X-Client-Request-Id</code> request header. This header isn’t added automatically; you must explicitly set it on the request.</p>
<p>When you include <code dir="auto">X-Client-Request-Id</code>:</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>You control the ID format (for example, a UUID or your internal trace ID), but it must contain only ASCII characters and be no more than 512 characters long; otherwise, the request will fail with a 400 error. Make this value unique per request.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>OpenAI logs this value internally for supported endpoints, including chat/completions, embeddings, responses, and more.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>In cases like timeouts or network issues when you can’t get the <code dir="auto">X-Request-Id</code> response header, you can share the <code dir="auto">X-Client-Request-Id</code> value with the support team to look up whether OpenAI received the request and when.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Example:</strong></p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="bash" dir="ltr"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;Authorization: Bearer </span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">$OPENAI_API_KEY</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;X-Client-Request-Id: 123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000&quot;</span></span></code></pre>
<div class="sl-heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="backwards-compatibility">Backwards compatibility</h2><a class="sl-anchor-link" href="#backwards-compatibility"><span aria-hidden="true" class="sl-anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span><span class="sr-only" data-pagefind-ignore="true">Section titled “Backwards compatibility”</span></a></div>
<p>OpenAI provides stability to API users by avoiding breaking changes in major API versions whenever reasonably possible. This includes:</p>
<ul>
<li>The REST API (currently <code dir="auto">v1</code>)</li>
<li>First-party <a href="/api/docs/libraries">client libraries</a> (released libraries adhere to <a href="https://semver.org/">semantic versioning</a>)</li>
<li><a href="/api/docs/models">Model</a> families (like <code dir="auto">gpt-4o</code> or <code dir="auto">o4-mini</code>)</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Model prompting behavior between snapshots is subject to change</strong>.
Model outputs are by their nature variable, so expect changes in prompting and model behavior between snapshots. The best way to ensure consistent prompting behavior and model output is to use pinned model versions, and to run <a href="/api/docs/guides/evals">evals</a> for your applications.</p>
<p><strong>Backwards-compatible API changes</strong>:</p>
<ul>
<li>Adding new resources (URLs) to the REST API and client libraries</li>
<li>Adding new optional API parameters</li>
<li>Adding new properties to JSON response objects or event data</li>
<li>Changing the order of properties in a JSON response object</li>
<li>Changing the length or format of opaque strings, like resource identifiers</li>
<li>Adding new event types in streaming APIs</li>
</ul>
<p>See the <a href="/api/docs/changelog">changelog</a> for a list of backwards-compatible changes and rare breaking changes.</p> </div>  </div>   </div>  </main>      </div> <!-- Note: padding-inline-end was just manually computed here.
